Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The logistic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 884 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 570 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the logistic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 884 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 570. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 400. QED.
